**[00:44:18.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000222)  
benefit the Southern Tagalog area, no, Calabar Zone, no, with the establishment of the UPPGH.

**[00:44:26.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000223)

**[00:44:28.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000224)

**[00:44:32.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000225)  
And of course, Ma'am Shirley Agor, please, I'm happy that you are now, ano, at least, ano, I'm sure, mas happy kami.

**[00:44:39.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000226)

**[00:44:54.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000229)

**[00:45:00.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000231)

**[00:45:05.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000232)

**[00:45:11.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000233)

**[00:45:22.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000235)

**[00:45:28.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000236)  
And again and again, lalabas at babalik. Pero nandito ho kami dahil mahalaga po sa amin ang higher education.

**[00:45:36.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000237)  
So magandang araw po muli. As we review the budget, we should all ask a very simple question.

**[00:45:43.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000238)  
Kapag nakapasok ang isang studyante sa kolehyo, may sapat na ba siyang suporta para makapagtapos?

**[00:45:51.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000239)  
Yun ho siguro ang isang tanong na kailangan ho nating harapin.

**[00:45:55.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000240)  
Sapat na ba ang nakukuha niyang suporta?

**[00:45:58.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000241)  
Sapat na ba ang nakukuha niyang suporta para makapagtapos?

**[00:46:00.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000242)  
Ibang issue pa po kung meron ba siyang trabaho pagka-graduate niya.

**[00:46:03.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000243)  
Dito muna tayo sa unang tanong.

**[00:46:06.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000244)  
Meron ba siyang sapat na suporta para makapagtapos?

**[00:46:10.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000245)  
Meron bang siguradong trabaho pagkatapos niya?

**[00:46:15.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000246)  
Alam po natin na napakalaking tulong ng libreng tuition.

**[00:46:19.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000247)  
Kaya po natin ito pinaglaban noong una nating termino.

**[00:46:22.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000248)  
Pero alam rin po natin na hindi doon nagtatapos ang gastos ng isang pamilya.

**[00:46:28.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000249)  
May pamasahe, pagkain, kagamitan at iba pang mga pangailangan sa pag-aaral.

**[00:46:34.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000250)  
Para sa ating nursing at allied health students,

**[00:46:37.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000251)  
meron pang dagdag na related learning experience na alam po natin napakabigat.

**[00:46:42.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000252)  
At mamaya po tatanungin ko rin kay Chair Shirley kung kailan po lalabas yung ating RLE support.

**[00:46:49.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000253)  
Na dapat po August 25 ba pong dapat lumabas.

**[00:46:52.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000254)  
Ito pong mga RLE ay mandatory.

**[00:46:54.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000255)  
Hindi po kayo pwedeng gumraduate sa nursing at allied health

**[00:46:57.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000256)  
kung hindi po kayo dumaan dito.

**[00:46:59.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000257)  
At kadalasan, tumataas rin po ang bayad ng RLE.

**[00:47:03.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000258)  
Umaabot po yan ng 20, 30, 40,000 pesos per year.

**[00:47:08.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000259)  
Hindi po dapat maging dahilan ang kakulangan sa pambayad

**[00:47:11.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000260)  
para hindi po makompleto ang kurso.

**[00:47:14.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000261)  
Noong nakaraang budget deliberation,

**[00:47:16.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000262)  
pinaglaban po natin ang pondo para sa libreng koleyo at tertiary education subsidy.

**[00:47:22.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000263)  
Ngayon pong pinag-uusapan ang 2027 budget,

**[00:47:25.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000264)  
sisiguraduhin po natin na sapat pa rin

**[00:47:27.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000265)  
ang pondo para sa libreng kolehiyo.

**[00:47:30.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000266)  
Habang dumarami ang mga estudyante at dumalawak ang saklaw ng batas,

**[00:47:34.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000267)  
hindi maaaring lumiit ang supportang direktang napupunta sa kanila.

**[00:47:39.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000268)  
Hindi dapat pabawasan ang pagkakatoon ng isang estudyante

**[00:47:41.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000269)  
ang makapasok at makapagtapos dahil lang kinapos ang budget.

**[00:47:46.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000270)  
Mr. Chairman, our previous chairperson, Senator Legarda,

**[00:47:50.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000271)  
actually ushered in the amendments to the Free College Act or RA 12325.

**[00:47:56.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000276)  
So, naiso nating malaman, itong mga bagong patakaran ba'y nai-implement na?

**[00:48:03.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000277)  
Ito ba'y nakakatulong sa pagpili ng mga estudyante

**[00:48:06.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000278)  
dapat makakuha ng narapat na tulong?

**[00:48:10.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000279)  
Kasama po sa mga amendments ang pagpasok ng RLE.

**[00:48:13.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000280)  
Hindi na po siya isang budget insertion or budget amendment.

**[00:48:16.960] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000281)  
Siya na po'y kasama na sa batas dahil po sa RA 12325.

**[00:48:21.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000282)  
12325 also clarified, sino yung mga batang dapat unang makakuha ng tulong

**[00:48:26.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000283)  
mula sa gobyerno.

**[00:48:27.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000284)  
And for the last budget, alam po natin that Chair Shirley and many here,

**[00:48:32.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000285)  
we agonized over the proper distribution of scholarships.

**[00:48:37.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000286)  
So, naiso nating malaman, with RA 12325 already passed and signed into law,

**[00:48:42.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000287)  
magkakasama po tayo nung pahirmahan po yan,

**[00:48:45.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000288)  
ito ba'y nilinaw na kung sinong estudyante ang dapat makakuha ng supporta,

**[00:48:50.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000289)  
saan lugar, anong klaseng institusyon, at paano ito aabot sa kanila.

**[00:48:55.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000290)  
So, nandito.

**[00:48:56.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000291)  
Nandito po tayo upang linawin ang bagong batas na RA 12325 while at the same time making sure

**[00:49:05.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000292)  
that our previous laws, the free college law, the UNIFAST law are all properly implemented.

**[00:49:12.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000293)  
Ang pinakahangarin po namin pagkatapos po ng budget hearing na ito ay malaman kung nasaan tayo

**[00:49:19.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000294)  
pagdating sa mga scholarships, pagdating sa mga subsidya, pagdating sa mga tulong sa mga estudyante,

**[00:49:25.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000295)  
and of course, later on, mapag-uusapan din ano yung tulong na kinakailangan ng CHED at ng SUC

**[00:49:30.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000296)  
na alam po natin ay parang nangalahati po sa kanilang request ang naibigay lang po sa kanila.

**[00:49:36.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000297)  
But alam naman po ito ni Chair Shirley, alam nyo po ito,

**[00:49:39.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000298)  
dito po sa Senado, marami pong sumusuporta sa CHED, sa mga SUC, kahit po sa mga LUC at mga pribadong institusyon,

**[00:49:47.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000299)  
we're here to support and to make sure ng bawat bata na makapasok, makapagtatapos,

**[00:49:52.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000300)  
at makakakuha ng tama at siguradong trabaho.

**[00:49:55.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000301)  
So yun lang po, Mr. Chairman, nagpapasalamat ako.

**[00:49:58.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000302)  
I know that you take the CHED budget very seriously, and we hope, dear colleagues, dear friends, we will be able to augment.

**[00:50:07.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000303)  
Inuunahan ko na, we hope to augment, we hope to increase, we hope to bolster, we hope to strengthen all of the budgets that go to you.

**[00:50:15.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000304)  
Dahil alam po namin, pag ito'y napupunta sa mga ahensya po na nandito, alam po namin, umaabot po ito sa mga estudyante na nangailangan.

**[00:50:22.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000305)  
Maraming salamat po at magandang hapon sa ating lahat.

**[00:50:25.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000306)  
Thank you,

**[00:50:25.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000306-split-2)  
thank you very much, Chairman Bam.

**[00:50:28.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000307)  
We badly need your help as we deal with this very challenging task.

**[00:50:35.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000308)  
Si Senator Bam has been our ally, especially in education pieces of legislation, advocacies, etc., being the chairperson of EDCOM 2.

**[00:50:49.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000309)

**[00:50:55.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000310)  
In today's budget briefing, and yung sinabi ni Sen Bam, totoo yun, napakaraming supporters dito ng CHED, ng SUCs, maging ng UP, kahit tinalo yung UST, okay pa rin, support pa rin tayo.

**[00:51:11.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000311)

**[00:51:49.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000316)

Thank you very much, Sir.

**[00:51:55.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000318)  
I hope hindi kasama ang greetings.

**[00:51:57.100] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000319)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating mahal na chairperson ng Committee on Finance and the subcommittee.

**[00:52:03.200] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000320)  
I, and of course, our champion, in many ways, Senator Bam Aquino, chairperson ng Basic Education.

**[00:52:11.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000321)

**[00:52:17.580] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000322)  
Marami pong salamat.

**[00:52:18.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000323)  
In the interest of time, I want to start with the message of Senator JV

**[00:52:25.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000324)  
Ejercito kanina sa PUP.

**[00:52:27.680] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000325)  
Sabi niya, mas magandang mag-invest sa edukasyon dahil ito ay panghanap buhay and that education will be our strongest weapon as we face the challenges of life.

**[00:52:40.960] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000326)  
Dito po kami nang gagaling.

**[00:52:42.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000327)  
After a year-long recalibration of our policies in CHED and redirecting everything, we are now ready to go far.

**[00:52:51.700] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000328)  
This is our basis when we crafted.

**[00:52:55.140] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000329)  
Our proposal for 2027, sir chairperson, our proposal was 70.2 billion, including HEDF, was cut to almost 50%.

**[00:53:12.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000330)  
If we can move to the next slide, please.

**[00:53:15.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000331)  
In the next slide, we can see that while we remain thankful at the education sector of the 7 trillion allotted.

**[00:53:24.380] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000332)  
For 2027, 1 trillion was given to education sector, but unfortunately and painfully, CHED only receive about 3.43% of the allocation in the education sector.

**[00:53:39.660] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000333)  
Moving forward, I have to run fast.

**[00:53:42.160] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000334)  
Moving forward, if you see the history on the next slide, from 2024 to 2025, palagi pong mas maliit ang natatanggap ng CHED.

**[00:53:58.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000336)  
And we are very thankful that upon assumption, we were given a budget of 48 billion, which is higher than the proposal.

**[00:54:07.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000337)  
This is a speaks volume of the growing support of our government to education sector.

**[00:54:13.540] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000338)  
So, this 2027, we carefully computed what we need after recalibrating everything.

**[00:54:22.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000339)  
this is not an imagination but this is need-based and data-driven need so we requested 70.2 billion

**[00:54:32.340] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000340)  
we only get 35.4 billion pesos if you go to the next slide just to show you the salient feature

**[00:54:40.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000341)  
of our 2027 CHED NEP. Saan po na-allot ang 35.4 billion na ibinigay? MOOE is 34.7 billion, which

**[00:54:52.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000342)  
represents 98%. Personnel services is 609 million. Capital outlay is 100 million.

**[00:55:00.900] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000343)  
po yung capital outlay kasi nakakapag-deliver po naman kami pero sa mooe if you pay attention

**[00:55:08.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000344)  
to that, the 34.7

**[00:55:10.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000345)  
billion, 98% goes to scholarship. Inuulit ko po ang aking rant: ang CHED

**[00:55:19.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000346)  
is not a scholarship agency but it is a regulatory and uh regulatory and developmental agency for

**[00:55:29.620] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000347)  
higher education if we move forward uh what is missing from the 2027 ched nep next slide please

**[00:55:38.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000348)  
uh this is the

**[00:55:40.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000349)  
miss uh gap we proposed for 70.2 billion they

**[00:55:44.900] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000350)  
gave 35.4 billion the gap is 34.8 billion saan po dapat nakalaan ang 34.8 billion if we

**[00:55:54.500] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000351)  
move to the next slide in summary yung priority one na min i i i describe it as critical non-deferable

**[00:56:02.820] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000352)  
pops as a 34.2 and then the priority which is the strategic higher education investment is

**[00:56:09.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000353)  
Ang kulang is 1.2, kaya dyan po napupunta. Ang kulang is 34.8 billion. Next slide. So ito po makikita natin dito ngayon ang ating diferensya kung saan ang makakaltasan po.

**[00:56:25.980] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000354)  
Sa priority 1, which include the scholarship from undergraduate, that is the UUACTE, and scholarship for advanced training of faculty, ang hiningi namin sa proposed namin is 66 billion. 34 ang binigay. Ang kulang namin is 31 billion pesos.

**[00:56:47.460] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000355)  
Sa strategic priority 2, we ask for 4 billion. Ang binigay po is 1.2.

**[00:56:54.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000356)  
Ang kailangan pa namin is 2.8 billion. Ang subtotal po ng naales ay 34.8 billion. After consulting with the state university and colleges, matagal na pong naghihirap ang SUC.

**[00:57:09.720] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000357)  
Since the enactment of this RA 10931, marami pong SUC ang nagkocollectan ng tuition fee from 12 pesos to 50 to 100 to 150 pesos. Ang dami pong challenges ngayon.

**[00:57:24.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000358)  
Tapos hanapan sila ng quality education paano? That's a big question. Kaya nilalagay namin ngayon yung ating dream proposed leveling up of tuition fee which is 7.5 billion. But I will discuss for now yung para sa CHED po.

**[00:57:42.840] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000359)  
So considering the fiscal space of the budget in the government, kami po ay inadvisean ng Congress to have a realignment of budget.

**[00:57:54.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000360)  
With the DSWD and the DBM and CHED. Kasi po ang 34 billion, ang 31 billion sa priority 1 ay 98% ay para sa yuwakte.

**[00:58:08.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000361)  
For you to appreciate, kung hindi ibibigay yung 17 billion after the recalibration, ang madidisplaced na estudyante is 940,000 students. Clear po yun.

**[00:58:24.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000362)  
Sir, could you repeat that?

**[00:58:25.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000363)  
Yung sa nung NEP namin po, we requested for a 66 billion to cover yuwakte and scholarship grant ng ating faculty. Pero ibinigay is 34.2 billion. Ang difference is 31.9 billion. Ang yuwakte, ang pupunta doon is 29.2 billion.

**[00:58:50.440] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000364)  
So considering the fiscal space, ang advice po.

**[00:58:54.760] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-000365)

**[01:59:29.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001744)

**[01:59:31.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001745)  
not only access,

but institutional

**[01:59:35.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001747)  
capacity, institutional

**[01:59:37.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001748)  
empowerment. And I think

**[01:59:39.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001749)  
if we will be avoiding

**[01:59:41.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001750)  
deficiency in our

**[01:59:43.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001751)  
SUCs, we will be guaranteed of

**[01:59:45.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001752)  
quality tertiary

**[01:59:47.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001753)  
education. In the next

**[01:59:49.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001754)  
slide, Mr. Chair, I think this is the question

**[01:59:51.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001755)  
of Senator Bam a while ago. This

**[01:59:53.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001756)  
is our illustration on the

**[01:59:55.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001757)  
computed acceptance

**[01:59:57.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001758)  
rate or admission rate.

**[01:59:59.020] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001759)  
Of the

**[01:59:59.920] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001760)  
1.3 million applied for admission, that's the demand, more than 500,000 or a little more than 500,000 was accepted.

**[02:00:07.600] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001761)  
So that is the 38.46% admission rate, Mr. Chair.

**[02:00:12.940] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001762)  
So the message from this number is unmistakable, Mr. Chair.

**[02:00:17.560] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001763)  
Demand for public higher education is already exceeding the capacity of our institutions to respond.

**[02:00:23.160] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001764)  
And I was very happy to listen to the conversation a while ago that to address this low admission rate, I think something must be done.

**[02:00:33.580] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001765)  
Deficiency must not be there because, you know, as in our illustration, 400 classrooms were not constructed because of the deficiency.

**[02:00:44.340] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001766)  
So that is, kung wala po sanang deficiency, baka na-expand ang ating capacity over the last four years.

**[02:00:50.080] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001767)  
Imagine the 12.3 billion, if that will be constructed.

**[02:00:53.160] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001768)  
If that will be concentrated for classroom, there will be more classroom and it will provide for a wider access.

**[02:00:58.340] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001769)  
So in the next slide, we are thankful that our students will get more support because of the expanded 10931.

**[02:01:09.460] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001770)  
We have now the 12325.

**[02:01:11.620] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001771)  
We acknowledge that the government continues to face competing demands for limited fiscal space, including social protection, health, basic education, and other essential services.

**[02:01:22.520] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001772)  
The challenge, however, is not to choose one vital public investment over the other, but to ensure that it receives adequate and sustainable funding commensurate with its mandate and the needs of its intended to address.

**[02:01:39.060] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001773)  
Now, Mr. Chair, what I am articulating is the expanded 10931. Republic Act 12325 provided expanded support for individual students.

**[02:01:50.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001774)  
But I think we must prioritize the deficiency before we allocate additional benefits.

**[02:01:57.940] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001775)  
Why not address?

**[02:01:59.120] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001776)  
Why are we allowing a deficiency in the allocation for free higher education?

**[02:02:05.040] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001777)  
In our app estimate, Mr. Chair, that is around 85% free.

**[02:02:09.780] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001778)  
We are not paid around 15%, while that is not the very tenet of 10931.

**[02:02:15.400] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001779)  
So I think what we plea, what we appeal, let us give full.

**[02:02:20.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001780)  
Funding to Republic Act 10931 so that our SUCs will no longer suffer these deficiencies.

**[02:02:26.420] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001781)  
This is, I may say, injustice to what we have promised them in terms of the full mandate of 10931.

**[02:02:37.920] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001782)  
Now, another plea, as earlier expressed, Mr. Chair, this is what we are really fighting for.

**[02:02:50.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001783)  
Section 54 of the Implementing Rules of Republic Act 10931 provides that the UniFAST Board must determine the composite fee.

**[02:02:59.400] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001784)  
Ito po yung tinalakay natin kanina with Senator Bam.

**[02:03:02.840] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001785)  
Now, or the normative composite fee.

**[02:03:04.840] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001786)  
Tapos na po si UniFAST.

**[02:03:07.120] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001787)  
At in fact, yung sinasabi kayo ng SGV, that is the firm contracted by UniFAST.

**[02:03:11.680] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001788)  
And based on the result, this is the formula considered.

**[02:03:15.700] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001789)  
There were series of consultation among SUCs in different clusters.

**[02:03:19.960] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001790)  
Indicators.

**[02:03:20.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001791)  
I think over the last year SGV proposed this normative formula.

**[02:03:26.580] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001792)  
The normative budget is computed as the standard salaries and wages plus the student based operating costs plus non-faculty personal costs plus repair and maintenance.

**[02:03:40.400] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001793)  
One beauty of this formula, this already includes miscellaneous fees, Mr. Chair.

**[02:03:44.800] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001794)  
It's not only cost of student tuition fee.

**[02:03:50.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001795)  
The initial result is around 1,212 pesos per unit.

**[02:03:55.360] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001796)  
And that is, I think, the per capita figure being asked by Senator Bam, that is 31,844 per student.

**[02:04:03.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001797)  
That on the average, opo, opo, opo, that includes already miscellaneous fees.

**[02:04:09.280] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001798)  
And I think if we can start with this, I think we must be serious in implementing this specific provisions of 10.931.

**[02:04:20.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001799)  
Now, because of the required finance or budget for this, PASUC also proposed alternative upgrade of our tuition fee.

**[02:04:34.580] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001800)  
As mentioned, as earlier articulated, there are SUCs collecting less than 100 peso, 50 peso, 12 peso.

**[02:04:42.880] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001801)  
This is our proposal for SUCs whose tuition fees are 100 pesos and below.

**[02:04:49.660] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001802)  
We propose.

**[02:04:50.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001803)  
100 or 200 peso increase per unit.

**[02:04:57.440] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001804)  
And for SUCs whose tuition fee is from 100 pesos to 300 pesos, we are proposing 150 pesos per unit increase.

**[02:05:10.620] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001805)  
There are, I think, there are 41 SUCs, Mr. Chair, among 113 collecting below 100 peso.

**[02:05:18.280] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001806)  
And there are one.

**[02:05:19.700] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001807)  
There are 70 SUCs collecting tuition fee from 101 to 300 peso per unit.

**[02:05:26.120] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001808)  
So for them, we propose an increase of 150 peso.

**[02:05:29.180] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001809)  
If you note, there is a calibration of increase because those who are collecting lesser must be increased higher.

**[02:05:36.820] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001810)  
So if this proposal will be taken, we will be requesting 13.37 billion pesos.

**[02:05:44.680] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001811)  
And for those SUCs collecting more than 300 to 500.

**[02:05:49.700] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001812)  
There must be a cap, no more than 500 peso.

**[02:05:53.300] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001813)  
Meaning, if you are collecting 400 peso already, you will be increased only of 100 peso.

**[02:05:58.180] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001814)  
So this is calibrated increase, Mr. Chair.

**[02:06:01.160] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001815)  
But if you are in that bracket, let's say, for example, you are collecting below 100 peso per unit, all of you will be increased by 200 peso per unit.

**[02:06:11.300] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001816)  
Now, I think this is more equitable in our view in PASUC.

**[02:06:16.520] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001817)  
But I think this is a little different from.

**[02:06:19.580] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001818)  
From what is presented by Ched a while ago.

**[02:06:21.920] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001819)  
But since there is difference in the presentation, what we really push for is the SGV formula.

**[02:06:28.120] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001820)  
We can agree.

**[02:06:29.460] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001821)  
We can agree on the trans.

**[02:06:31.920] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001822)  
So medyo, this is just an alternative proposal.

**[02:06:36.300] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001823)  
We agree with Ched that if we can talk with DBM and of course, Ched and SUCs, let's agree.

**[02:06:43.480] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001824)  
But let's make it more scientific, more evidence-based.

**[02:06:47.720] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001825)  
Kasi itong amin pong ito, actually.

**[02:06:49.580] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001826)  
This is just an assumption, a step gap, the one is just to make it equivalent or uniform, lahat 250.

**[02:06:59.880] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001827)  
Kung 250 ka na, ba't sumisingil ka ng 240, ang increase mo na lang 10 peso.

**[02:07:03.760] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001828)  
Ba't ang tanong, is that scientific?

**[02:07:05.760] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001829)  
So to avoid discussion on this, what we propose is the SGV formula, a calibrated SGV formula.

**[02:07:12.840] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001830)  
So that accounts to 13.37 billion, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:07:17.120] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001831)  
Next slide.

**[02:07:19.580] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001832)  
So I think, Mr. Chair, I would like to summarize our ask or our request, our plea.

**[02:07:26.840] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001833)  
The very reason why the 113 SUCs came here today, because of the following.

**[02:07:34.200] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001834)  
First is to settle the validated FHE deficiency for fiscal year 2025 and fiscal year 2026, estimated at around 5 billion pesos.

**[02:07:46.200] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001835)  
That is our very first priority.

**[02:07:49.580] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001836)  
Second is to provide additional appropriation for capital outlay, especially for 25 SUCs without infrastructure allocation in the 2027 NEP.

**[02:08:04.200] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001837)  
That is to expand our capacity and to increase, of course, the quality of our education in our SUCs.

**[02:08:12.040] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001838)  
Third, Mr. Chair, is to implement, seriously.

**[02:08:16.380] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001839)  
the

**[02:08:17.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001840)  
article or the section

**[02:08:20.680] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001841)  
54 of the implementing rules

**[02:08:22.760] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001842)  
of the IRR or the IRR

**[02:08:24.840] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-001843)

**[02:18:22.320] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002088)

**[02:18:23.320] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002089)  
We are supposed to lead in academic standards, teaching, and faculty development and research.

**[02:18:29.260] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002090)

**[02:18:33.780] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002091)  
serve as a regional and global university for the entire country, and we are supposed

**[02:18:38.060] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002092)  
to promote leadership and responsible citizenship.

**[02:18:41.360] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002093)

**[02:18:43.260] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002094)

**[02:18:46.080] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002095)

**[02:18:50.420] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002096)  
They will decay and be a waste.

**[02:18:52.500] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002097)

**[02:18:53.220] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002098)  
the enhancement of UP

**[02:18:54.160] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002099)  
Mindanao School of Medicine

**[02:18:55.240] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002100)  
and the development

of the Archipelagic

**[02:18:57.560] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002102)  
and Oceanic University

**[02:18:58.580] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002103)

**[02:18:59.300] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002104)

**[02:18:59.800] UP President Angelo A. Jimenez** (asr-002105)

**[02:30:46.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002431)

**[02:30:58.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002432)

**[02:31:12.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002433)

**[02:31:21.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002434)  
And they were saying they can only, I don't want to...

**[02:31:28.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002435)  
I want to make sure that I have the right exact words that they told me about.

**[02:31:33.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002436)

**[02:31:56.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002437)  
on Wednesday.

**[02:31:58.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002438)

**[02:32:11.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002439)

**[02:32:12.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002440)  
Thursday, bumiyahin ulit sila, the same day, the same flight, the same airline, and two hours again before the flight, sinabi, cancelled.

**[02:32:21.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002441)  
So, you know, I'm thinking of, well, I have filed a bill here in the Senate for consumerism.

**[02:32:28.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002442)

**[02:32:42.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002443)

**[02:32:51.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002444)

**[02:32:56.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002445)

**[02:32:58.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002446)

**[02:33:06.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002447)

**[02:33:26.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002448)  
Same exact thing.

**[02:33:27.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002449)

**[02:33:56.980] UP Resilience Institute Executive Director Alfredo Mahar Francisco A. Lagmay** (asr-002455)  
Ang tawag po.

**[02:33:57.640] UP Resilience Institute Executive Director Alfredo Mahar Francisco A. Lagmay** (asr-002456)  
Sir, sa akin, lahar magmay.

**[02:34:02.380] UP Resilience Institute Executive Director Alfredo Mahar Francisco A. Lagmay** (asr-002457)  
Anyway, yes, sir, nakukonsult po kami. In fact, for DPWH, even before nabigay sa amin yung aming SARO, January pa lang ay kinukonsultan na po kami ng DPWH.

**[02:34:16.520] UP Resilience Institute Executive Director Alfredo Mahar Francisco A. Lagmay** (asr-002458)  
In fact, they have been giving us their designs for projects, thousands of them, na tinitignan namin kung okay o hindi.

**[02:34:25.860] UP Resilience Institute Executive Director Alfredo Mahar Francisco A. Lagmay** (asr-002459)  
And tinitignan namin kung worth it yung ini-invest ng peso ng LGU or ng national government doon sa project.

**[02:34:34.540] UP Resilience Institute Executive Director Alfredo Mahar Francisco A. Lagmay** (asr-002460)  
And this has happened only this year after there has been a lot of parang issues regarding the flood control.

**[02:34:46.440] UP Resilience Institute Executive Director Alfredo Mahar Francisco A. Lagmay** (asr-002461)  
Masama din po ang Congress and Senate na may mga pinatitignan din sa amin na yung mga design kukunin din namin from DPWH.

**[02:51:01.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002663)

**[02:51:09.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002664)

**[02:51:27.900] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002667)

**[02:51:47.600] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002668)

**[02:51:57.580] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002670)

**[02:52:00.960] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002671)

**[02:52:05.480] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002672)

**[02:52:10.840] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002673)

**[02:52:17.800] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002674)

**[02:52:24.400] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002675)

**[02:52:29.040] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002676)

**[02:52:34.380] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002677)

**[02:52:42.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002678)

**[02:52:46.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002679)

**[02:52:51.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002680)  
Which is MOE rin yun?

**[02:52:53.000] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002681)  
Hindi po. So ang zero balance billing po ay to pay for services.

**[02:52:59.660] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002682)  
Ang MOE po kailangan namin i-bid.

**[02:53:01.980] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002683)  
Kunwari may gamot, bibili kami ng medication, ng drugs, chemicals, reagents,

**[02:53:07.160] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002684)  
or the other supplies needed for patients.

**[02:53:11.320] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002685)  
Yung zero balance billing po is paying for the service.

**[02:53:14.220] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002686)  
Kunwari po ay pinasiti-scan or MRI.

**[02:53:16.960] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002687)  
Or PET-CT scan.

**[02:53:18.540] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002688)

**[02:53:22.060] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002689)  
Yung service po na ito, ito yung babayaran ng zero balance billing.

**[02:53:27.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002690)  
Nasa MOE rin siya, diba? Yung zero balance billing?

**[02:53:30.020] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002691)  
Nandun rin po siya, pero may special po siyang kategorya.

**[02:53:33.920] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002692)  
Para hindi po siya kailangan i-bid out for drugs, medicines, and supplies.

**[02:53:39.860] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002693)

**[02:53:46.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002694)

**[02:54:01.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002695)  
So in your case, sa PGH, itong zero balance billing na idinagdag ng Senate, is this enough or is it kulang?

**[02:54:10.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002696)

**[02:54:14.840] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002697)

**[02:54:16.960] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002698)

**[02:54:25.960] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002699)  
So tama lang po itong, kung mabalik lang po yung aming 800 million na shortfall for our submitted post-budget for 2027 billion,

**[02:54:41.000] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002700)  
we will be able to carry out the zero balance billing program for 2027.

**[02:54:45.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002701)

**[02:54:46.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002702)  
For the zero balance billing.

**[02:54:49.960] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002703)  
It's around 2 billion pesos a year po.

**[02:54:52.080] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002704)  
2 billion pesos a year.

**[02:54:53.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002705)  
And then of which, ang nabigay?

**[02:54:56.560] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002706)

**[02:54:59.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002707)  
1.3, so may balance sa 700.

**[02:55:00.780] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002708)  
Opo.

**[02:55:02.540] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002709)  
Pagka po lang kukulang, ginagamit ng hospital ang income or savings para po tuloy-tuloy na mabigay ang servisyo sa zero balance billing.

**[02:55:13.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002710)  
Opo.

**[02:55:13.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002711)  
Kasi yun ang...

**[02:55:15.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002712)  
Of course, yun ang ating...

**[02:55:16.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002713)  
battle cry right now, yung zero balance billing.

**[02:55:21.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002714)  
So yung mga constituents natin na pumupunta sa public hospitals,

**[02:55:26.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002715)  
regardless of your income,

**[02:55:28.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002716)  
you can avail of the zero balance billing.

**[02:55:32.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002717)  
So yun yung we're trying to protect that for 2027.

**[02:55:37.060] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002718)  
If I may explain, Mr. Chair,

**[02:55:39.660] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002719)  
ang zero balance billing po ngayon ay 100% sa ating mga indigent patients.

**[02:55:46.080] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002720)  
So,

**[02:55:46.960] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002721)  
meron po kaming meeting ng PhilHealth at ng DOH na paano pag-aralan na iaakyat po ito sa mga lower middle income patients or even the middle income patients which actually expands universal health care coverage.

**[02:56:00.600] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002722)  
So, meron pong mga programa tulad ng EMA-EFIP.

**[02:56:05.000] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002723)  
Pagka dinagdag po natin sa current funding ng PhilHealth, ng income ng hospital, ng savings,

**[02:56:12.840] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002724)  
we can now see a path towards expanding zero balance.

**[02:56:16.960] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002725)  
Billing to those already in the pay wards, hindi naman sa pay single room pero nasa ward po, and hopefully mga teachers ang maliit lang ang sweldo o ating mga government workers ang maliit lang ang sweldo pero nasa pay floors, nasa pay wards, mako-cover na lang po hopefully ng zero balance billing or at least konti na lang ang kanilang ilalabas. So yun po yung pinag-aaralan ngayon.

**[02:56:41.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002726)  
I think that's also,

**[02:56:44.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002727)  
Right now, kasi kung nasa pay ward ka,

**[02:56:46.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002728)  
ang pumapasok is yung

**[02:56:48.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002729)  
MAIP.

**[02:56:49.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002731)  
Yung MAIP.

**[02:56:51.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002732)  
Yung medical assistance, MAIP, yun ang pumapasok.

**[02:56:55.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002733)  
But if you're outside of the pay ward, dito na pumapasok si zero balance billing.

**[02:57:02.440] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002734)  
Correct.

**[02:57:03.380] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002735)  
Pero kung kayo po, kahit may kaya kayo, ang prinsipi ng zero balance billing, kahit may kaya kayo at kayo humiga sa isang charity bed, zero pa rin ho kayo.

**[02:57:14.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002736)  
Correct.

**[02:57:14.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002737)  
That's where we are balancing kasi since uncovered lang naman ng zero balance ay yung charity ward or the pay ward.

**[02:57:27.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002738)  
Marami kasing cases sa mga government hospitals dahil sobrang puno.

**[02:57:33.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002739)  
Ang occupancy rate ng government hospital naglalaro anything between 130% to almost 200% in some cases.

**[02:57:42.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002740)  
Nafaforce sila, napapwere sa sila.

**[02:57:44.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002741)  
Sa pay wards.

**[02:57:47.260] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002742)  
Opo.

**[02:57:47.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002743)  
Pag na-forsa sila sa pay wards, hindi naman sila maka-avail ng zero balance billing.

**[02:57:53.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002744)  
So doon na pumapasok yung medical assistance or MAIP.

**[02:57:59.360] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002745)  
Yung bagong MAIP, yung bagong programa, ito po ay as end users, mas madali po namin nagagamit ito kasi po ang approving authority lang ay yung medical center chief.

**[02:58:12.220] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002746)  
So mas na-review.

**[02:58:14.700] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002747)  
Para-rationalize po namin ang paggamit ng e-MAIP with the actual need, with the actual financial burden of the patient.

**[02:58:23.460] PGH Director Dr. Gerardo D. Legaspi** (asr-002748)  
Dahil nga social worker based pa rin yung pag-allot ng assistance sa mga private patient na alanganin naman pong magbayad din.

**[02:58:32.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002749)  
Well, binabalansi po namin dito nila Senator Joel yung MAIP, yung zero balance billing as well as yung PhilHealth subsidies.

**[02:58:43.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002750)  
So all of this.

**[02:58:44.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002751)  
Kasi dumadating dito so we're balancing that eh no?

**[02:58:48.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002752)  
Opo, yeah.

**[02:59:31.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002761)

**[02:59:56.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002764)  
Yung actual kasi creates a deficiency.

**[03:00:00.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002765)

**[03:00:18.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002766)  
Thank you very much, sir.

**[03:00:48.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002767)  
May check eh. Itong mga 20 ay may konting deficiency pa. So, if they can submit it next week, then we will finish the disbursement immediately.

For the second question.

**[03:01:11.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002771)

**[03:01:18.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002772)

**[03:01:21.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002773)

**[03:01:43.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002774)  
For the second question.

**[03:01:45.960] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002775)  
Yan po yung pinagkakaroon.

**[03:01:48.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002776)

**[03:01:54.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002777)

**[03:01:57.800] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002778)  
We already have the data.

**[03:01:59.940] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002779)

**[03:02:06.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002780)

**[03:02:13.600] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002781)

**[03:02:22.560] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002785)

**[03:02:48.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002792)  
Tuition fee of all 113 SUCs and LUCs, the cost per unit is P1,212 per unit.

**[03:03:04.180] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002793)

**[03:03:18.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002794)

**[03:03:27.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002795)

**[03:03:33.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002796)  
So, we came up with different options, proposals.

**[03:03:39.420] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002797)

**[03:03:41.260] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002798)  
Kasi po, hindi po talaga kaya.

**[03:03:43.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002799)  
We are all aware of the limited fiscal space of the government.

**[03:03:48.300] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002800)

**[03:11:11.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002916)

**[03:11:15.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002917)

**[03:11:25.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002919)

**[03:11:32.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-002920)

**[03:12:07.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002934)

**[03:12:17.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002935)

**[03:12:46.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002944)  
Si Dr. Sorieta wants to…

**[03:12:48.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002945)  
Mr. Chair, to add some insight to the discussion.

**[03:12:52.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002946)  
Tama po kayo na kung sinusunod lang po ang allocation basis sa projected enrollment, wala pong deficiency.

**[03:13:01.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002947)  
Alam po ninyo sa 2027, by the time we speak, siguradong meron ng deficiency.

**[03:13:08.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002948)  
Kasi 46 among our SUCs now are allocated the same as the allocation for FHC in 2026.

**[03:13:17.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002949)  
Ngayon po, by the time we speak, meaning to say, wala na tayong room ngayon for increase.

**[03:13:23.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002950)  
Kasi ang budget na ibinigay sa SUC, 46 SUCs, for 2027 sa FHC,

**[03:13:29.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002951)  
kuneope lang po yung budget nila ng FHC ng 2026.

**[03:13:33.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002952)  
So hindi pa po ginagamit, which I think that's in the special provision,

**[03:13:37.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002953)  
that the allocation must be determined by the projected enrollment.

**[03:13:44.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002954)  
Ngayon, hindi ko rin po alam kung why DBM is not following that.

**[03:13:49.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002955)  
Sabi nila, ayaw nilang sundin yung binigay naming projected enrollment kasi wala raw basis.

**[03:13:56.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002956)  
Saan ba natin generally

**[03:13:58.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002957)  
kukuhanin ang projected enrollment?

**[03:14:01.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002958)  
It goes without saying that when you project enrollment, you will count the number of your chairs.

**[03:14:07.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002959)  
But in counting the number of chairs, we must also count the digital chairs, the digital seats.

**[03:14:12.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002960)  
It's not peg must be to the physical chairs.

**[03:14:16.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002961)  
The number of teachers, or even modality, or even number of shifts.

**[03:14:22.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002962)  
Kung morning shifts, afternoon shifts, meron pa ding evening shifts ng klase.

**[03:14:27.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002963)  
Ang number of shifts, modality.

**[03:14:29.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002964)  
I have some reservation on the formula being introduced to determine the absorptive capacity.

**[03:14:35.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002965)  
Tanong po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:14:37.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002966)  
Sa 10931, kasi ang aming pong reservation dyan sa absorptive capacity, baka gamitin yan as a cap of our enrollment

**[03:14:47.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002967)  
if we will be counting the number of seats and the number of faculty.

**[03:14:51.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002968)  
Now, where shall we base our absorptive capacity?

**[03:14:56.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002969)  
To our current limitation or to the demand?

**[03:15:00.000] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002970)  
Let's say, for example, just to give context to our discussion,

**[03:15:04.260] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002971)  
this 2025, there are 1.3 million students who sought admission to our SUCs.

**[03:15:10.820] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002972)  
And only 500,000 were admitted, meaning we were not able to admit 800,000 students.

**[03:15:20.700] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002973)  
Now, if we will be stretching our capacity, let's say, for example, a bit,

**[03:15:25.800] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002974)  
baka maging 600,000 sana.

**[03:15:27.400] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002975)  
Now, let's say, for example, ako last year, my enrollment, let's say, for example, is 60,000.

**[03:15:34.240] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002976)  
Maybe it's safe for me to say na I can stretch by finding more or looking for more faculty.

**[03:15:40.600] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002977)  
I may endeavor to find more chairs next school year.

**[03:15:45.900] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002978)  
I should not be limited by my last year's number of chairs.

**[03:15:50.820] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002979)  
I must not be limited by my last year's number of teachers because I can hire teachers.

**[03:15:57.400] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002980)  
Because of the smart campuses that we have now,

**[03:16:00.260] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002981)  
some of our SUCs can deliberate blended to increase the capacity.

**[03:16:04.840] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002982)  
I have some opportunity to talk to, let's say, Arizona State.

**[03:16:08.420] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002983)  
And I asked them, what is your number of students?

**[03:16:27.400] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002988)

**[03:16:39.980] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002991)

**[03:16:42.600] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002992)

**[03:16:47.740] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002993)

**[03:16:59.820] PASUC President Dr. Tirso A. Ronquillo** (asr-002997)

ng number of seats, number of teachers, number of shifts,

**[03:18:32.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003031)

**[03:18:38.360] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003034)  
Enrollment projection, I believe, is a function of the absorptive capacity of university.

**[03:18:44.740] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003035)

**[03:18:52.920] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003036)  
Number one is the student-faculty ratio.

**[03:18:55.240] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003037)

**[03:18:57.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003038)

**[03:19:01.000] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003039)

**[03:19:17.520] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003040)

**[03:19:27.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003041)

**[03:19:37.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003042)

**[03:19:38.320] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003043)

**[03:19:49.040] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003044)

**[03:19:57.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003045)

**[03:20:01.780] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003046)

**[03:20:08.400] CHED Chairperson Shirley Castañeda Agrupis** (asr-003047)
